# Agents · P · part 8

983 entries, pitch-judge to plan-proposer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pitch-judge**: LAST agent in the /pitch kill gate, and in /adopt's verdict. Reads an anonymised case file — the settled answers, the search reports (for /adopt, a verbatim survey report as well), and two unattributed verdicts — and returns its own independent verdict of build, kill or park. Knows nothing about who produced which verdict. Runs once, automatically, never on demand. Does NOT search, does NOT read files, does NOT suggest improvements to the idea. ([konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook](https://github.com/konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/pitch-judge.md) · ★3)
- **pitch-modeler**: pitch-modeler worker for pitch-agent ([iTzFaisal/financial-services](https://github.com/iTzFaisal/financial-services/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/pitch-modeler.md))
- **pitch-prep**: Full pitch preparation workflow. Researches the prospect, audits competitors, gathers source material, builds a creative brief, and generates a proposal. Use when preparing for a new business pitch or responding to an RFP. ([camawjones/creativestack](https://github.com/camawjones/creativestack/blob/HEAD/agents/pitch-prep.md) · ★2)
- **pitch-researcher**: Ecosystem and competitor researcher for the pitch skill. Identifies the closest competitors to a project, reads their actual READMEs, docs, and changelogs, and returns a cited feature matrix plus positioning observations as structured data. Use when the pitch skill (or any ideation flow) needs verified competitive intelligence rather than search-snippet guesswork. ([cjmellor/mella-marketplace](https://github.com/cjmellor/mella-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/mella/agents/pitch-researcher.md))
- **pitch-researcher**: Desk-research specialist for client/market intelligence. Use when a skill or the team needs sourced research on a company, its executives, its marketing track record, competitors, or category — e.g. pitch-brief-compass context research, competitor scans, market checks. Returns raw structured findings with source discipline, not prose for humans. ([dongwu711/pitch-brief-compass](https://github.com/dongwu711/pitch-brief-compass/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pitch-researcher.md))
- **pitch-researcher**: pitch-researcher worker for pitch-agent ([iTzFaisal/financial-services](https://github.com/iTzFaisal/financial-services/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/pitch-researcher.md))
- **pitch-researcher**: Codebase and context archaeology for a Haven & Way feature being shaped. Given a raw feature idea, finds what already exists that touches it — sections of the page, routes, config, prior pitches and decisions, and above all the thing that already does most of the job — and returns a short digest. Use from the shaper skill so file reading stays out of the conversation. ([mikefsm/haven_and_way](https://github.com/mikefsm/haven_and_way/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pitch-researcher.md))
- **pitch-review-assessor**: Assess sales pitch quality from three stakeholder perspectives (buyer, sales, marketing). ([cogni-work/insight-wave](https://github.com/cogni-work/insight-wave/blob/HEAD/cogni-sales/agents/pitch-review-assessor.md) · ★15)
- **pitch-reviewer**: Reviews a startup pitch deck / executive summary for investor-readiness — fabricated or unverifiable claims, weak or missing "why now"/ask, inconsistent numbers, and generic boilerplate. Use when the user asks to review, critique, or sanity-check a pitch deck, exec summary, or fundraising doc (e.g. "review this deck", "check this pitch for gener8tor", "is this ready to send investors"). ([cadavidf/automality](https://github.com/cadavidf/automality/blob/HEAD/plugins/automality/agents/pitch-reviewer.md))
- **pitch-revisor**: Revise sales pitch deliverables based on pitch-review-assessor feedback. ([cogni-work/insight-wave](https://github.com/cogni-work/insight-wave/blob/HEAD/cogni-sales/agents/pitch-revisor.md) · ★15)
- **pitch-scout**: Read-only codebase analysis scout for the pitch skill. Scans a project's architecture, API surface, tests, dependencies, docs, and DX friction, then returns ranked, evidenced findings as structured data. Use when the pitch skill (or any ideation flow) needs a cheap, fast codebase survey without spending frontier-model tokens on file reading. ([cjmellor/mella-marketplace](https://github.com/cjmellor/mella-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/mella/agents/pitch-scout.md))
- **pitch-shaper**: Takes a rough idea, lists its open decisions for the main session to ask, and produces a Shape-Up pitch with appetite, scopes, rabbit holes, no-gos, and circuit breaker. - **pitchbook-orchestrator**: Routes financial questions to the right PitchBook MCP tools (103 tools across 13 modules). Uses intent matching to narrow 103 tools to 3-5 candidates, then selects. Shifts into 8 category prompt modules based on question type. Use when: user asks about companies, investors, deals, funds, LPs, market data, deal signals, valuations, due diligence, outreach prep, board materials, or any PitchBook data query. Also use for background signal polling and pipeline monitoring. ([Lua2147/claude-toolkit-catalog](https://github.com/Lua2147/claude-toolkit-catalog/blob/HEAD/agents/pitchbook-orchestrator.md) · ★2)
- **pitchd-rails-implementor**: Implements a single plan task or scoped feature in a Rails app using the implementing-pitchd-rails skill: rails-omakase-compass (DHH / 37signals shape) plus writing-* skills and rules/*.mdc; may use referencing-unofficial-37signals-guide for supplemental Fizzy-derived topics or referencing-rails-guides for authoritative Rails API docs when plugin material is insufficient. Writes code and tests, verifies, and reports — does not commit. Parent must paste full task text, context, and work directory — subagent has no prior chat history. ([Pitchd-Ltd/pitchd-rails-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/Pitchd-Ltd/pitchd-rails-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/pitchd-rails-implementor.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **pitchd-rails-primitives-maintainer**: Runs capture, trace, update, or lint passes against the primitives tree under docs/primitives/ using maintaining-primitives: capability docs with intent clauses, shape constraints, evaluation maps, and append-only provenance, plus the human-owned compilation.md. Use when delegating a focused primitives session — backfilling a historical feature, answering a provenance question, syncing a doc, or a tree health pass — without Rails app implementation. Parent must paste operation, paths, and constraints — subagent has no prior chat history. ([Pitchd-Ltd/pitchd-rails-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/Pitchd-Ltd/pitchd-rails-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/pitchd-rails-primitives-maintainer.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)

DHH / 37signals perspective (omakase, server-owned truth, REST gravity, Hotwire-first, boring code). Readonly; explains and recommends — does not implement or commit unless the parent explicitly asks for code in the same turn. - **pkc3-smoker**: PKC3 の**実ブラウザ smoke を、対象範囲だけ**回す専門エージェント。「触った物 → どの動線が変わるか」を名指しし、その spec だけを走らせて結果を返す。🔴 **必ず worktree 隔離(isolation="worktree")で起動する**(`npm run build` が `dist/` を書き換えるため)。user 指示 2026-09-11「基本的には単体テストを自分で行い、サブエージェントに対象範囲のみのスモークテストをさせろ / UI導線のテストをフルスモークで誤魔化すな」の実体。 ([sm06224/PKC3](https://github.com/sm06224/PKC3/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pkc3-smoker.md))
- **pkc3-surveyor**: PKC3 リポジトリの read-only 実地調査員。着手判断・設計 doc・バグ調査の材料集めに使う。file:line で根拠を示し、実装量(S/M/L)とリスクまで返す。コードは一切変更しない。 ([sm06224/PKC3](https://github.com/sm06224/PKC3/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pkc3-surveyor.md))
- **pkc3-ux-reviewer**: PKC3 を user の立場で読む read-only の UX レビュアー。「その場で user は何を見て、どう受け取り、次にどこを押すか」を物語の形で検証し、動線の欠陥と改善案を具体的な推奨つきで出す。コードは一切変更しない。 ([sm06224/PKC3](https://github.com/sm06224/PKC3/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pkc3-ux-reviewer.md))

- **plain-prose-reviewer**: Checks text written for the owner (an issue body, a spec, a PRD section, demo copy, a PR body, or a long chat reply) for phrasing that forces a second read. Flags a sentence where an idea is the subject of a verb, a trailing clause whose meaning the text never states, thesis nouns, a short phrase that packs a whole clause, a pronoun with nothing to refer to, a rule the text implies but never says, and a message that puts its point last.
